Procedure.i Hash(p.i,n.i)
 Define i.i,h.i
 h=2166136261
 For i=0 To n-1 : h=((h ! PeekA(p+i))*16777619)&$FFFFFFFF : Next
 ProcedureReturn h
EndProcedure
